**530.N** Refer any risk with a swimming pool lacking visible safety controls. Do not bind until the hazard is evaluated.

Pool exposures require reliable barriers and maintenance practices. Review confirms that loss controls are present and acceptable.

Record the pool condition and the referral basis. Note required corrective action.

**530.O** Refer any risk with a diving board, slide, or similar pool feature. Do not assume ordinary pool controls address the added hazard.

Special pool features may increase bodily injury severity. Review determines whether the feature is acceptable.

Record the feature and the underwriting disposition. Retain any required condition.

**530.P** Refer any risk with a trampoline. Do not bind without underwriting review.

Trampolines can create significant bodily injury exposure. Review confirms whether the hazard fits carrier appetite.

Record the trampoline exposure and the referral outcome. Note any required removal or control.

**530.Q** Refer any risk with an unfenced water feature. Do not rely on verbal assurances regarding supervision.

Open water features may present an attractive nuisance hazard. Review determines whether adequate protections are in place.

Record the water feature and observed safeguards. Document the underwriting decision.

**530.R** Refer any risk with a recreational vehicle stored or operated at the premises. Do not treat vehicle activity as incidental without review.

Recreational vehicles may create injury, theft, and liability concerns. Review confirms the nature of use and storage.

Record the vehicle type, use, and referral outcome. Retain underwriting notes.

**530.S** Refer any risk with an unregistered vehicle stored on the premises. Do not disregard inoperable vehicle conditions.

Stored vehicles can indicate premises maintenance concerns or unreported exposure. Review determines whether the condition is acceptable.

Record the vehicle condition and the referral basis. Note the underwriting disposition.

**530.T** Refer any risk with visible debris, discarded materials, or neglected grounds. Do not bind until property condition is assessed.

Poor maintenance may signal increased fire, liability, or moral hazard concerns. Review supports consistent property condition standards.

Record the observed condition and referral outcome. Note required corrective action.

**530.U** Refer any risk with unrepaired roof damage. Do not accept assurances of future repair without review.

Roof condition affects water intrusion and structural loss potential. Review confirms whether repairs are adequate and timely.

Record the roof condition and the underwriting disposition. Retain repair information when available.

**530.V** Refer any risk with exterior deterioration. Do not disregard damaged siding, trim, steps, or railings.

Exterior deterioration may create weather, liability, and maintenance concerns. Review determines whether the property remains acceptable.

Record the observed condition and referral basis. Note any required correction.

**530.W** Refer any risk with unsafe stairs, porches, decks, or railings. Do not bind pending evaluation of the hazard.

Unsafe walking surfaces can create substantial bodily injury exposure. Review confirms that required repairs are completed.

Record the unsafe condition and the underwriting outcome. Retain condition requirements.

**530.X** Refer any risk with unsecured openings. Do not bind where doors or windows cannot provide reasonable protection.

Unsecured openings may increase theft, vandalism, and weather loss potential. Review confirms property security and condition.

Record the opening condition and referral disposition. Note required repairs.

**530.Y** Refer any risk with evidence of water intrusion. Do not treat staining or active leakage as cosmetic damage.

Water intrusion may indicate hidden deterioration or repeated loss potential. Review determines whether the source has been corrected.

Record the affected area and reported repair status. Document the underwriting decision.

**530.Z** Refer any risk with a prior plumbing leak that lacks repair confirmation. Do not bind based on an unverified repair statement.

Plumbing losses can recur where repairs are incomplete or temporary. Review confirms that the source of loss has been addressed.

Record the reported loss cause and repair information. Retain referral direction.

**530.AA** Refer any risk with visible electrical deficiencies. Do not bind while electrical condition remains uncertain.

Electrical deficiencies can increase fire and shock hazards. Review determines whether repairs and inspection are satisfactory.

Record the observed deficiency and referral outcome. Note required corrective action.

**530.AB** Refer any risk with exposed wiring or unsafe electrical panels. Do not accept the exposure without review.

Unsafe electrical components may present immediate fire potential. Review confirms that the property condition meets underwriting standards.

Record the electrical condition and underwriting disposition. Retain repair confirmation when available.

**530.AC** Refer any risk with heating equipment in poor condition. Do not bind until the equipment is evaluated.

Poorly maintained heating equipment may increase fire or carbon monoxide exposure. Review confirms safe operation and maintenance.

Record the heating condition and the referral basis. Note any required repair.

**530.AD** Refer any risk using portable heating as a primary heat source. Do not assume temporary equipment is suitable for ongoing use.

Portable heating can increase fire and burn exposure. Review determines whether the heating arrangement is acceptable.

Record the reported heating arrangement and the underwriting outcome. Retain relevant notes.

**530.AE** Refer any risk with solid-fuel heating equipment. Do not bind without review of installation and maintenance.

Solid-fuel heating can create elevated fire and chimney exposure. Review confirms that safeguards are adequate.

Record the equipment type and referral disposition. Note required risk controls.

**530.AF** Refer any risk with an inoperable chimney or flue. Do not accept the risk until the condition is addressed.

Defective chimneys and flues may increase fire and smoke damage potential. Review confirms whether use has ceased or repairs are complete.

Record the condition and the underwriting decision. Retain repair information.

**530.AG** Refer any risk with fuel storage concerns. Do not disregard leaking containers or unsafe placement.

Fuel storage may create fire, pollution, and property damage exposure. Review determines whether storage practices are acceptable.

Record the observed fuel condition and referral outcome. Note corrective requirements.

**530.AH** Refer any risk with a generator installation. Do not bind without evaluating installation and fuel arrangements.

Generators may create fire, carbon monoxide, and electrical hazards. Review confirms that the equipment is safely maintained.

Record the generator information and underwriting disposition. Retain any required conditions.

**530.AI** Refer any risk with a backup power system showing poor maintenance. Do not assume standby equipment is inactive.

Backup systems can present fuel, electrical, and fire exposure. Review confirms the equipment condition and intended use.

Record the system condition and referral basis. Note the underwriting outcome.

**530.AJ** Refer any risk with a security system represented as active but not verified. Do not apply related consideration without support.

Security features affect theft and property protection assumptions. Review confirms that the represented protection is reliable.

Record the verification status and underwriting action. Note any removed consideration.

**530.AK** Refer any risk with fire protection information that is incomplete or inconsistent. Do not bind until the information is resolved.

Protection information supports evaluation of fire response and property exposure. Review prevents reliance on inaccurate risk details.

Record the missing or inconsistent information and referral outcome. Retain final underwriting direction.

**530.AL** Refer any risk with a location that presents access concerns. Do not assume emergency response is adequate without review.

Limited access may affect fire response and loss severity. Review determines whether the location remains acceptable.

Record the access concern and underwriting disposition. Note any supporting risk information.

**530.AM** Refer any risk with a waterfront location. Do not bind without evaluating the surrounding exposure.

Waterfront conditions may increase wind, water, erosion, or liability potential. Review confirms fit with carrier appetite.

Record the location characteristic and referral outcome. Retain relevant underwriting notes.

**530.AN** Refer any risk with signs of shoreline erosion. Do not disregard conditions affecting foundations or access.

Erosion may lead to structural damage and changing property conditions. Review determines whether the exposure is acceptable.

Record the observed condition and referral basis. Note the underwriting disposition.

**530.AO** Refer any risk with a retaining wall showing distress. Do not bind until structural concerns are evaluated.

Retaining wall failure may create property damage and liability exposure. Review confirms whether repairs are needed.

Record the wall condition and referral outcome. Retain required corrective action.

**530.AP** Refer any risk with foundation cracking or movement. Do not treat structural concerns as routine cosmetic conditions.

Foundation conditions may indicate settlement, water, or structural issues. Review determines whether the risk remains acceptable.

Record the reported condition and underwriting decision. Note repair status when available.

**530.AQ** Refer any risk with visible structural alteration. Do not assume alterations were completed safely.

Alterations may affect construction, valuation, and property stability. Review confirms the nature and status of the work.

Record the alteration and referral disposition. Retain available supporting information.

**530.AR** Refer any risk undergoing renovation. Do not bind until occupancy and construction conditions are reviewed.

Renovation can increase fire, theft, weather, and liability exposure. Review determines whether the risk is eligible during the work.

Record the renovation status and underwriting outcome. Note required conditions.

**530.AS** Refer any risk with construction materials stored at the premises. Do not disregard materials that increase fire or theft exposure.

Stored materials may indicate ongoing work or unsafe premises conditions. Review confirms whether the exposure is temporary or continuing.

Record the materials observed and referral basis. Note the underwriting disposition.

**530.AT** Refer any risk with contractor activity at the premises. Do not treat contractor presence as ordinary maintenance without review.

Contractor activity can change liability, vacancy, and property conditions. Review determines whether the work affects eligibility.

Record the reported activity and referral outcome. Retain underwriting direction.

**530.AU** Refer any risk with a disputed ownership interest. Do not bind while insurable interest remains unclear.

Uncertain ownership can affect eligibility, claims handling, and policy issuance. Review confirms the proper insured relationship.

Record the ownership concern and underwriting disposition. Note the basis for acceptance or decline.

**530.AV** Refer any risk with a trust, estate, or similar ownership arrangement. Do not assume ordinary ownership treatment applies.

Special ownership arrangements may require review of insurable interest and occupancy. Review confirms proper risk setup.

Record the ownership arrangement and referral outcome. Retain underwriting direction.

**530.AW** Refer any risk occupied by persons other than the named household. Do not assume household status without review.

Occupancy arrangements can affect liability, property use, and eligibility. Review determines whether the occupancy fits carrier appetite.

Record the reported occupants and underwriting disposition. Note any restrictions required.

**530.AX** Refer any risk with tenant occupancy. Do not bind property coverage based on owner occupancy assumptions.

Tenant occupancy changes premises control and loss exposure. Review confirms the intended coverage treatment.

Record the tenancy information and referral outcome. Retain relevant notes.

**530.AY** Refer any risk with room rental activity. Do not treat rented rooms as ordinary household occupancy.

Room rental may increase visitor, liability, and property exposure. Review determines whether the activity is acceptable.

Record the rental activity and underwriting disposition. Note any required action.

**530.AZ** Refer any risk with shared ownership occupancy. Do not bind until the occupancy arrangement is understood.

Shared occupancy may create uncertainty regarding control, maintenance, and insurable interest. Review confirms proper risk handling.

Record the occupancy arrangement and referral basis. Retain the underwriting outcome.

**530.BA** Refer any risk with inconsistent mailing and premises information. Do not disregard unexplained address differences.

Address inconsistencies may indicate occupancy, ownership, or disclosure concerns. Review confirms the correct risk location and use.

Record the inconsistent information and referral outcome. Note the resolution.

**530.BB** Refer any risk with material information that conflicts across carrier records. Do not select the most favorable description without review.

Conflicting information may affect classification, valuation, or eligibility. Review ensures that the accepted exposure is accurately described.

Record the conflicting information and underwriting disposition. Retain the confirmed risk description.

**530.BC** Issue a notice of intent not to renew only after underwriting direction is recorded. Handle any notice for nonpayment through the approved servicing process.

Notice handling requires accurate status and clear underwriting support. Proper documentation promotes consistent carrier action.

Record the reason for the action and the underwriting direction. Retain the servicing status in the risk record.

## Rule 540 — Louisiana State Exceptions

**540.A** Decline any risk with roof age at or above 20 years.

Roof condition can materially affect wind and water loss potential.

Document roof age support and the declination reason in the underwriting file.

**540.B** Bind Coverage A only within line underwriter authority of $500,000.

Risks above delegated authority require review at the appropriate authority level.

Document the authorized reviewer and approval in the underwriting file.

**540.C** Do not write Coverage A above $750,000.

Risks above the Coverage A ceiling are outside Louisiana appetite.

Document the declination reason or approved exception in the underwriting file.

**540.D** Refer any risk with evidence of unrepaired roof damage.

Unrepaired damage may indicate an active loss exposure.

Document the roof condition, source of information, and referral outcome.

**540.E** Refer any risk with missing roof age information.

Roof age is required to evaluate roof condition and wind exposure.

Document efforts to obtain roof age support and the final disposition.

**540.F** Refer any risk with conflicting roof age information.

Conflicting information prevents reliable evaluation of roof eligibility.

Document the conflicting sources and the accepted roof age basis.

**540.G** Decline any risk with visible roof deterioration that affects weather resistance.

Deteriorated roofing may increase the likelihood of wind-driven rain damage.

Document the observed condition and declination reason in the underwriting file.

**540.H** Refer any risk with roof repairs that appear incomplete.

Incomplete repairs may leave the dwelling vulnerable to weather damage.

Document the repair status and requested evidence of completion.

**540.I** Refer any risk with evidence of repeated roof patching.

Repeated patching may indicate a roof nearing the end of useful service.

Document the roof condition and underwriting decision in the file.

**540.J** Refer any risk with roof material not readily identifiable.

Roof material affects evaluation of wind and hail susceptibility.

Document the available roof information and requested verification.

**540.K** Refer any risk with roof geometry that may increase wind susceptibility.

Complex roof features can create elevated wind uplift exposure.

Document the roof characteristics and referral outcome.

**540.L** Refer any risk with an attached structure showing separate roof deterioration.

Attached structures can contribute to water intrusion and property damage.

Document the attached structure condition and underwriting action.

**540.M** Decline any risk with active water intrusion.

Active water intrusion indicates unresolved maintenance or damage conditions.

Document the source of water intrusion and declination reason.

**540.N** Refer any risk with prior water damage that lacks repair support.

Prior water damage may indicate continuing moisture or structural concerns.

Document the loss information and available repair evidence.

**540.O** Refer any risk with signs of mold, rot, or decay.

These conditions may indicate prolonged moisture exposure or deferred maintenance.

Document the observed condition and required underwriting action.

**540.P** Refer any risk with foundation movement indicators.

Foundation movement can affect structural integrity and future loss potential.

Document the observed indicators and referral disposition.

**540.Q** Refer any risk with exterior wall damage.

Exterior damage may permit wind-driven rain or further property deterioration.

Document the condition and required repair information.

**540.R** Refer any risk with unsecured exterior features.

Unsecured features can become windborne debris during severe weather.

Document the feature, condition, and underwriting decision.

**540.S** Refer any risk with damaged or missing exterior shutters.

Exterior opening protection affects wind-driven rain exposure.

Document the opening protection condition and requested correction.

**540.T** Refer any risk with damaged windows or exterior doors.

Damaged openings may increase susceptibility to wind and water damage.

Document the condition and evidence of repair.

**540.U** Refer any risk with an unprotected opening exposed to weather.

Weather-exposed openings may allow water intrusion during severe conditions.

Document the opening condition and underwriting action.

**540.V** Refer any risk with visible chimney deterioration.

Chimney damage can indicate maintenance concerns and wind vulnerability.

Document the chimney condition and referral outcome.

**540.W** Refer any risk with evidence of vacant occupancy.

Vacant property may have delayed loss discovery and reduced maintenance.

Document occupancy information and the underwriting decision.

**540.X** Refer any risk with seasonal or intermittent occupancy.

Intermittent occupancy may affect inspection, maintenance, and loss reporting.

Document occupancy use and the approved underwriting basis.

**540.Y** Refer any risk with tenant occupancy not supported by the requested policy type.

Occupancy must align with the intended coverage and eligibility requirements.

Document occupancy status and the final policy disposition.

**540.Z** Refer any risk used for business activity that affects residential exposure.

Business activity may increase liability, property, or traffic exposure.

Document the business use and referral result.

**540.AA** Refer any risk with short-term rental activity.

Rental turnover may increase property wear and liability exposure.

Document the rental activity and underwriting disposition.

**540.AB** Refer any risk with commercial vehicles regularly kept at the location.

Commercial vehicle presence may indicate business activity or increased premises exposure.

Document vehicle use and the underwriting decision.

**540.AC** Refer any risk with recreational equipment that creates elevated premises exposure.

Recreational equipment may increase liability severity or frequency.

Document the equipment and applicable underwriting action.

**540.AD** Refer any risk with a trampoline.

A trampoline presents an elevated bodily injury exposure.

Document the trampoline status and referral outcome.

**540.AE** Refer any risk with a diving board or water slide.

These features may increase swimming pool liability exposure.

Document the feature and the underwriting decision.

**540.AF** Refer any risk with a swimming pool lacking secure access controls.

Secure access controls help reduce unauthorized use and injury exposure.

Document the pool condition and required corrective action.

**540.AG** Refer any risk with an unfenced pool area.

Unrestricted pool access may increase liability exposure.

Document the pool access condition and referral result.

**540.AH** Refer any risk with a dog history that indicates elevated liability exposure.

Animal behavior history may affect liability eligibility.

Document the disclosed animal information and underwriting action.

**540.AI** Refer any risk with exotic, agricultural, or inherently hazardous animals.

Such animals may create liability exposures outside personal lines appetite.

Document the animal type and referral disposition.

**540.AJ** Refer any risk with a prior liability loss involving an animal.

Prior animal-related losses may indicate continuing liability exposure.

Document the loss information and final underwriting decision.

**540.AK** Refer any risk with prior fire loss information.

Prior fire losses may indicate unresolved property or maintenance concerns.

Document the loss details and the underwriting rationale.

**540.AL** Refer any risk with prior wind or hail loss information.

Wind and hail loss history may indicate property condition or location concerns.

Document the loss information and referral outcome.

**540.AM** Refer any risk with prior water loss information.

Water loss history may indicate plumbing, roof, drainage, or maintenance concerns.

Document the loss details and underwriting action.

**540.AN** Refer any risk with repeated property loss activity.

Repeated losses may indicate an exposure outside normal underwriting appetite.

Document the loss history and final disposition.

**540.AO** Refer any risk with an open claim.

Open claims may leave property condition or loss responsibility unresolved.

Document the claim status and referral decision.

**540.AP** Refer any risk with a prior claim lacking sufficient detail.

Incomplete loss information prevents reliable evaluation of future exposure.

Document the information requested and the final underwriting basis.

**540.AQ** Refer any risk with undisclosed prior losses discovered during review.

Undisclosed losses may affect eligibility and rating accuracy.

Document the discrepancy and underwriting disposition.

**540.AR** Refer any risk with material differences between the application and inspection information.

Material differences may affect eligibility, rating, or coverage selection.

Document the discrepancy and corrective action.

**540.AS** Refer any risk with an inspection showing deferred maintenance.

Deferred maintenance may increase the likelihood or severity of loss.

Document the inspection findings and required underwriting action.

**540.AT** Refer any risk with unsafe electrical conditions.

Electrical deficiencies may create fire and property damage exposure.

Document the condition and evidence of correction.

**540.AU** Refer any risk with unsafe heating equipment.

Unsafe heating equipment may increase fire or carbon monoxide exposure.

Document the equipment condition and referral outcome.

**540.AV** Refer any risk with fuel storage that presents a premises hazard.

Fuel storage may increase fire, pollution, or liability exposure.

Document the storage condition and underwriting decision.

**540.AW** Refer any risk with evidence of plumbing leaks.

Plumbing leaks may cause recurring water damage or hidden deterioration.

Document the leak condition and required repair evidence.

**540.AX** Refer any risk with damaged drainage features.

Poor drainage may increase water intrusion and foundation exposure.

Document the drainage condition and corrective action.

**540.AY** Refer any risk with a history of flooding or surface water entry.

Flooding and surface water entry may indicate exposure not addressed by the requested coverage.

Document the history and underwriting disposition.

**540.AZ** Refer any risk with a backwater valve concern.

A backwater valve condition may affect sewer and drain loss exposure.

Document the valve information and referral outcome.

**540.BA** Refer any risk where the maximum windstorm and hail deductible percentage is not available for selection.

Deductible availability must support the applicable Louisiana underwriting requirements.

Document the available deductible options and underwriting action.

**540.BB** Refer any risk submitted outside the named storm window.

Timing restrictions may affect binding eligibility for storm-sensitive risks.

Document the submission status and final disposition.

**540.BC** Apply credits on the state exception pages of the rating manualcredits on the state exception pages of the rating manual (see Homeowners and Dwelling Rating Manual, P12 Part 12 — State Exception Pages) only when supported by the required risk information.

Credits must reflect verified risk characteristics and approved rating treatment.

Document the supporting information and applied credit basis.

**540.BD** Refer any risk requesting an exception to Louisiana eligibility criteria.

Exceptions require review before binding or issuing coverage.

Document the requested exception, supporting facts, and approval outcome.

**540.BE** Retain underwriting support for every Louisiana exception decision.

Complete documentation supports consistent application of state-specific underwriting requirements.

Document the final eligibility, authority, rating, and exception decisions in the underwriting file.

## Rule 550 — North Carolina State Exceptions

**550.A** Refer any risk with Coverage A above $700,000. Do not bind pending authority review.  
This preserves delegated authority controls. It prevents unsupported commitments.  
Document the proposed Coverage A and referral disposition. Record the approving authority in the underwriting file.  

**550.B** Require a roof inspection before binding where roof age is at or above 18 years. Hold binding action pending review of the inspection.  
Roof condition may affect acceptability. Inspection evidence supports a consistent decision.  
Document the roof inspection source and condition findings. Retain the inspection record with the risk file.  

**550.C** Refer risks with unresolved ownership information. Do not bind while the insurable interest remains unclear.  
Clear ownership supports proper underwriting. Unresolved interests may create claim handling issues.  
Document the ownership information reviewed. Record the referral outcome and any conditions imposed.  

**550.D** Verify that the named insured has an insurable interest in the dwelling. Refer discrepancies between occupancy information and ownership information.  
Accurate insured identification supports contract administration. Inconsistent information may indicate an unacceptable exposure.  
Document the ownership evidence reviewed. Record any clarification obtained from the producer or applicant.  

**550.E** Refer risks with occupancy descriptions that conflict across submitted information. Do not bind until the occupancy classification is resolved.  
Occupancy affects eligibility and hazard evaluation. Conflicting descriptions require underwriting review.  
Document the conflicting information. Record the accepted occupancy classification and basis for acceptance.  

**550.F** Refer risks with business activity conducted at the residence. Do not assume incidental activity is acceptable.  
Business activity may increase liability, property, or traffic exposure. The activity requires review for eligibility.  
Document the nature of the activity and area used. Record the underwriting decision and any restrictions.  

**550.G** Refer risks with care services provided at the residence. Do not bind where the service creates an unreviewed liability exposure.  
Care operations may create exposures outside the intended personal lines profile. Review supports proper placement.  
Document the service description and attendance pattern. Record the referral disposition in the risk file.  

**550.H** Refer risks with rental activity not clearly reflected in the occupancy description. Do not rely on verbal assumptions regarding tenant use.  
Rental activity may change the hazard and coverage profile. Clear use information supports accurate underwriting.  
Document the rental use and dwelling occupancy. Record the basis for the eligibility determination.  

**550.I** Refer risks with short-term lodging activity. Do not bind pending review of the frequency and nature of guest use.  
Guest turnover may increase property damage and liability exposure. The activity may fall outside standard eligibility.  
Document the lodging activity description. Record the authority decision and any required action.  

**550.J** Refer risks with vacant or unoccupied conditions. Do not bind unless the condition has been reviewed for acceptability.  
Lack of regular occupancy may delay discovery of damage. It may also increase maintenance concerns.  
Document the reported occupancy status. Record the reason for vacancy or unoccupancy and underwriting action.  

**550.K** Refer risks with construction, major repair, or renovation activity. Do not bind until the work scope is understood.  
Construction activity may affect property protection and liability exposure. The work may alter dwelling condition or value.  
Document the work description and current condition. Record the decision and any binding conditions.  

**550.L** Refer risks with structural damage that has not been repaired. Do not bind based solely on an applicant representation of future repair.  
Existing damage may indicate an active loss condition. It may also impair the dwelling's protective features.  
Document the damage description and supporting evidence. Record the repair status and underwriting disposition.  

**550.M** Refer risks with prior water damage or active moisture concerns. Do not bind until the source and repair status are evaluated.  
Water conditions may result in recurring property damage. Unresolved moisture may indicate deterioration.  
Document the source, repairs, and present condition. Record the basis for acceptance, decline, or referral.  

**550.N** Refer risks with visible deterioration affecting the dwelling exterior. Do not bind where maintenance condition cannot be confirmed.  
Exterior condition may indicate broader upkeep concerns. Deterioration can increase susceptibility to loss.  
Document the observed condition and information source. Record the action taken and any required remediation.  

**550.O** Refer risks with unsafe premises conditions. Do not bind while a material hazard remains unresolved.  
Unsafe conditions may create foreseeable property or liability losses. Review protects against unsupported acceptance.  
Document the condition and location on the premises. Record the corrective action required or referral decision.  

**550.P** Refer risks with unrepaired fire damage. Do not bind until restoration status and cause information are reviewed.  
Fire damage may impair structural integrity and protective systems. Cause information may affect acceptability.  
Document the damage scope and reported cause. Record the restoration status and underwriting decision.  

**550.Q** Refer risks with unrepaired wind, hail, or storm damage. Do not bind pending review of current condition.  
Storm damage may expose the dwelling to further loss. Unrepaired conditions may indicate an active claim issue.  
Document the damage description and repair evidence. Record the decision and supporting rationale.  

**550.R** Refer risks with unresolved foundation concerns. Do not bind where structural stability is uncertain.  
Foundation conditions may affect the dwelling's integrity. Uncertainty may create material property exposure.  
Document the reported concern and available inspection evidence. Record the underwriter's disposition.  

**550.S** Refer risks with prior sinkhole, earth movement, or settlement concerns. Do not bind until the condition has been evaluated.  
Ground movement may produce recurring structural damage. Prior conditions may indicate continuing exposure.  
Document the reported history and present condition. Record the review outcome and any limitations.  

**550.T** Refer risks with detached structures used for commercial, manufacturing, or storage activity. Do not classify the structure as ordinary personal use without review.  
Nonresidential use may create unanticipated property and liability exposure. The use may affect eligibility.  
Document the structure use and contents description. Record the underwriting decision in the file.  

**550.U** Refer risks with agricultural activity on the premises. Do not bind until the activity and related structures are reviewed.  
Agricultural operations may present hazards outside the intended profile. Related equipment and animals may increase exposure.  
Document the activity, structures, and premises use. Record the referral disposition.  

**550.V** Refer risks with animals that present a liability concern. Do not bind while animal information remains incomplete.  
Animal ownership may create material bodily injury exposure. Underwriting review supports consistent handling.  
Document the animal information provided. Record the acceptance decision and any applicable condition.  

**550.W** Refer risks with exotic, wild, or restricted animals. Do not rely on a general pet description.  
Such animals may create severity concerns beyond ordinary household exposures. Clear identification is necessary for evaluation.  
Document the animal type and housing conditions. Record the authority decision.  

**550.X** Refer risks with a prior liability allegation involving the premises or household. Do not bind until the circumstances are reviewed.  
Prior allegations may indicate an unresolved liability pattern. Review supports appropriate risk selection.  
Document the allegation summary and disposition. Record the underwriting basis for the final action.  

**550.Y** Refer risks with reported criminal activity associated with the premises. Do not make eligibility assumptions from limited information.  
Reported activity may affect property, liability, or moral hazard evaluation. A complete review is required.  
Document the reported activity and information source. Record the referral result and supporting notes.  

**550.Z** Refer risks with material misrepresentation indicators. Do not bind where submitted information cannot be reconciled.  
Accurate information is necessary for sound risk selection. Misrepresentation indicators require heightened review.  
Document the discrepancy and verification efforts. Record the referral disposition.  

**550.AA** Verify protective device information before applying any related treatment. Refer claims of protection that cannot be supported.  
Protective features affect hazard evaluation. Unsupported information may lead to inaccurate underwriting.  
Document the device information and verification source. Record any correction made to the risk record.  

**550.AB** Refer risks with security, fire protection, or alarm information that conflicts with inspection findings. Do not bind pending reconciliation.  
Conflicting protection information may materially affect exposure assessment. Inspection findings require consideration.  
Document the conflicting details. Record the final protection classification and basis.  

**550.AC** Refer risks with a dwelling location that is difficult for emergency access. Do not assume ordinary fire response.  
Access limitations may affect loss severity. Emergency response conditions require review.  
Document the access concern and information source. Record the underwriting decision.  

**550.AD** Refer risks with limited water supply information. Do not bind if fire protection conditions cannot be evaluated.  
Water supply conditions may affect fire exposure. Clear information supports consistent risk assessment.  
Document the available water supply information. Record the authority action.  

**550.AE** Refer risks with prior cancellation, nonrenewal, or refusal information that is not explained. Do not bind until the reason is understood.  
Prior adverse action may identify underwriting concerns. The reason may affect current acceptability.  
Document the prior action and reported explanation. Record the review outcome.  

**550.AF** Refer risks with loss information that is incomplete, inconsistent, or unavailable. Do not assume favorable loss experience.  
Loss history is material to risk evaluation. Missing information may conceal unresolved exposure.  
Document the available loss information and verification efforts. Record the underwriting action.  

**550.AG** Refer risks with repeated water-related loss indications. Do not bind until cause and remediation are reviewed.  
Recurring water loss may indicate a continuing condition. Repair evidence supports a defensible decision.  
Document the reported loss causes and corrective work. Record the basis for acceptance or referral.  

**550.AH** Refer risks with prior fire loss indications. Do not bind until cause, repairs, and present condition are reviewed.  
Prior fire loss may indicate unresolved hazards. Restoration and cause information are material to acceptability.  
Document the loss information and repair status. Record the referral disposition.  

**550.AI** Refer risks with prior theft or vandalism loss indications. Do not bind until property protection and circumstances are reviewed.  
Theft and vandalism may reflect location or protection concerns. Review supports appropriate underwriting treatment.  
Document the loss circumstances and protective measures. Record the decision in the file.  

**550.AJ** Refer risks with prior weather-related loss indications that remain unresolved. Do not bind while repair status is uncertain.  
Unrepaired weather damage may lead to additional loss. The dwelling condition must be evaluated.  
Document the reported loss and current repair status. Record the underwriting outcome.  

**550.AK** Refer risks with mortgagee information that conflicts with ownership information. Do not issue coverage with unresolved interested-party details.  
Accurate interested-party information supports proper policy administration. Conflicts may delay claim handling.  
Document the information reviewed and discrepancy identified. Record the resolved information in the risk file.  

**550.AL** Refer requests to change the fungi and bacteria aggregate. Do not alter the treatment without authority review.  
The fungi and bacteria aggregate requires controlled handling. Unauthorized changes may create unintended exposure.  
Document the requested change and stated reason. Record the authority decision.  

**550.AM** Apply credits on the state exception pages of the rating manualcredits on the state exception pages of the rating manual (see Homeowners and Dwelling Rating Manual, P12 Part 12 — State Exception Pages) only when the supporting conditions are verified. Do not apply a credit from unverified information.  
State-specific rating treatment requires accurate support. Verification promotes consistent premium handling.  
Document the verified basis for the credit. Record the source used for validation.  

**550.AN** Refer requests that would exceed the seacoast deductible ceiling. Do not offer an exception without authority approval.  
The seacoast deductible ceiling is subject to controlled administration. Referral prevents unauthorized treatment.  
Document the requested deductible treatment and referral result. Record the approving authority.  

**550.AO** Refer risks with coastal exposure concerns that are not adequately described. Do not bind until location characteristics are reviewed.  
Coastal conditions may increase weather-related exposure. Accurate location information supports proper handling.  
Document the location details and exposure concerns. Record the underwriting decision.  

**550.AP** Refer risks with prior flood, tidal water, or storm surge damage indications. Do not bind until the circumstances are understood.  
Water-related events may indicate significant location exposure. Prior damage may affect current condition.  
Document the reported event and repair status. Record the referral disposition.  

**550.AQ** Refer risks with drainage concerns affecting the residence. Do not bind while water diversion conditions remain unresolved.  
Poor drainage may contribute to repeated property damage. Site conditions require review for acceptability.  
Document the drainage concern and corrective measures. Record the underwriter's action.  

**550.AR** Refer risks with a history of sewer, drain, or sump discharge. Do not bind until cause and mitigation are reviewed.  
Discharge losses may recur without effective correction. Mitigation information supports sound selection.  
Document the loss cause and remediation status. Record the final disposition.  

**550.AS** Refer risks with plumbing conditions that may create an active leak exposure. Do not bind where repair status is uncertain.  
Plumbing defects may cause sudden or gradual damage. Current condition must be established.  
Document the condition and reported repairs. Record the basis for the decision.  

**550.AT** Refer risks with electrical conditions that may create a fire hazard. Do not bind until the condition has been reviewed.  
Electrical deficiencies may increase fire severity. Review supports safe risk selection.  
Document the reported condition and corrective work. Record the underwriting outcome.  

**550.AU** Refer risks with heating equipment concerns. Do not bind where installation, maintenance, or condition information is incomplete.  
Heating equipment may create fire or carbon monoxide exposure. Complete information is necessary for evaluation.  
Document the equipment concern and available support. Record the authority decision.  

**550.AV** Refer risks with solid fuel heating equipment. Do not bind until installation and maintenance information are reviewed.  
Solid fuel heating may create elevated fire exposure. Proper review supports consistent underwriting.  
Document the equipment type and installation details. Record the referral result.  

**550.AW** Refer risks with fuel storage concerns on the premises. Do not bind until the exposure has been evaluated.  
Fuel storage may increase fire, pollution, or liability exposure. Location and condition are material.  
Document the storage information and condition. Record the underwriting disposition.  

**550.AX** Refer risks with a pool, spa, or similar water feature lacking clear safety information. Do not bind pending review of the premises condition.  
Water features may create significant liability exposure. Safety controls require evaluation.  
Document the water feature and available safety information. Record the decision and conditions.  

**550.AY** Refer risks with recreational equipment that may create liability exposure. Do not assume ordinary personal use resolves the concern.  
Certain equipment may increase injury exposure. The use and storage conditions require review.  
Document the equipment description and usage. Record the underwriting action.  

**550.AZ** Refer risks with trampolines, elevated play equipment, or similar features. Do not bind until liability exposure is reviewed.  
Recreational features may create bodily injury exposure. Premises safeguards may affect acceptability.  
Document the feature and safety conditions. Record the referral outcome.  

**550.BA** Refer risks with unusual liability hazards on the premises. Do not bind where the hazard cannot be clearly described.  
Unusual hazards may fall outside expected personal lines exposure. Clear facts are necessary for evaluation.  
Document the hazard description and location. Record the authority decision.  

**550.BB** Refer risks with hazardous materials stored or used on the premises. Do not bind until the material and use are evaluated.  
Hazardous materials may create property, bodily injury, or pollution exposure. Storage conditions may affect severity.  
Document the material, use, and storage conditions. Record the underwriting disposition.  

**550.BC** Refer risks with environmental contamination allegations. Do not bind pending review of cause, extent, and remediation.  
Contamination allegations may create substantial uncertainty. Remediation status is material to acceptability.  
Document the allegation and available remediation information. Record the referral result.  

**550.BD** Refer risks with easement, access, or boundary disputes. Do not bind while a material premises dispute remains unresolved.  
Property disputes may affect liability and claim handling. Clear premises control is necessary.  
Document the dispute description and affected area. Record the underwriting action.  

**550.BE** Refer risks with shared access, shared driveways, or shared premises features. Do not assume responsibility is clear.  
Shared features may create maintenance and liability concerns. Control of the area requires evaluation.  
Document the shared feature and responsible parties. Record the basis for acceptance or referral.  

**550.BF** Refer risks with unusual architectural features that may affect replacement cost or repairability. Do not bind without adequate valuation support.  
Special construction may increase repair complexity. Accurate valuation supports sound underwriting.  
Document the feature and valuation information. Record the authority disposition.  

**550.BG** Refer risks with historic, landmark, or preservation restrictions. Do not bind until repair obligations are understood.  
Restrictions may affect reconstruction cost and repair methods. The impact may be material to valuation.  
Document the restriction information and property characteristics. Record the underwriting decision.  

**550.BH** Refer risks with unusual building materials. Do not bind until durability, repairability, and hazard concerns are reviewed.  
Nonstandard materials may affect loss severity and reconstruction. Underwriting requires sufficient support.  
Document the building material and information source. Record the final action.  

**550.BI** Refer risks with a roof condition that conflicts with applicant information. Do not bind pending resolution of the discrepancy.  
Roof condition is material to weather-related exposure. Conflicting information requires reliable support.  
Document the discrepancy and evidence reviewed. Record the accepted roof condition.  

**550.BJ** Refer risks with roof repairs that appear incomplete. Do not bind based on an unsupported representation of completion.  
Incomplete repairs may leave the dwelling vulnerable to loss. Current condition must be established.  
Document the repair information and condition evidence. Record the underwriting disposition.  

**550.BK** Refer risks with prior inspection findings that remain open. Do not bind until the condition has been resolved or accepted by authority.  
Open findings may identify material hazards. Authority review is required before acceptance.  
Document the finding and present status. Record the required action or approval.  

**550.BL** Refer risks with underwriting conditions that have not been satisfied. Do not release coverage pending unresolved conditions.  
Conditions support the basis for acceptance. Unmet conditions may invalidate the original evaluation.  
Document the outstanding condition and follow-up efforts. Record the final status.  

**550.BM** Refer requests for exceptions to stated underwriting requirements. Do not grant an exception without delegated authority.  
Exceptions require controlled decision-making. Authority review promotes consistent risk selection.  
Document the requested exception and supporting facts. Record the approving authority or decline reason.  

**550.BN** Refer risks submitted with incomplete material information. Do not fill material gaps through assumptions.  
Incomplete submissions may produce inaccurate underwriting decisions. Required facts must be available for evaluation.  
Document the missing information and request for clarification. Record the disposition after review.  

**550.BO** Verify material applicant statements when information appears inconsistent or unusual. Refer the risk if verification cannot be obtained.  
Verification supports reliable risk selection. Unconfirmed statements may create avoidable exposure.  
Document the information verified and source used. Record unresolved items and referral action.  

**550.BP** Refer risks with an address that cannot be matched to the insured premises description. Do not bind until the location is confirmed.  
Correct location information is necessary for property and territory evaluation. Address discrepancies may affect rating and eligibility.  
Document the address discrepancy and resolution. Record the confirmed premises location.  

**550.BQ** Refer risks with mailing information that differs from residence information without explanation. Do not assume ordinary occupancy.  
Different addresses may indicate occupancy or ownership concerns. Clarification supports accurate classification.  
Document the addresses provided and explanation received. Record the underwriting decision.  

**550.BR** Refer risks with trust, estate, or entity ownership. Do not bind until the named insured and insurable interest are reviewed.  
Nonstandard ownership may affect eligibility and claim administration. Proper party identification is required.  
Document the ownership structure and supporting information. Record the authority disposition.  

**550.BS** Refer risks with a change in ownership during the underwriting process. Do not bind using superseded ownership information.  
Ownership changes may affect eligibility and interested-party details. The file must reflect current facts.  
Document the change and updated ownership evidence. Record the final insured information.  

**550.BT** Refer risks with a change in occupancy before binding. Do not rely on prior occupancy representations.  
Occupancy changes may alter hazard characteristics. Current use must support the underwriting decision.  
Document the change and revised occupancy description. Record the action taken.  

**550.BU** Refer risks with a change in dwelling condition before binding. Do not issue coverage without reviewing the changed condition.  
Condition changes may materially affect exposure. Current information supports a defensible decision.  
Document the change and supporting evidence. Record the underwriting disposition.  

**550.BV** Refer risks with new loss information received before binding. Do not proceed until the information has been evaluated.  
New loss information may affect acceptability. Timely review prevents reliance on outdated assumptions.  
Document the loss information and review date. Record the resulting underwriting action.  

**550.BW** Refer risks with producer-submitted information that differs from applicant-submitted information. Do not select favorable information without verification.  
Conflicting submissions require reconciliation. Verification supports consistent underwriting treatment.  
Document the conflicting statements and verification results. Record the accepted information.  

**550.BX** Refer risks with inspection information that differs from application information. Do not bind until the discrepancy is resolved.  
Inspection evidence may identify material hazard conditions. The risk record must reflect supported facts.  
Document the discrepancy and inspection source. Record the final underwriting decision.  

**550.BY** Refer risks with valuation information that appears inadequate for the dwelling characteristics. Do not bind until the valuation is reviewed.  
Inadequate valuation may result in unsupported coverage placement. Dwelling characteristics must be considered.  
Document the valuation concern and information reviewed. Record the authority action.  

**550.BZ** Refer risks with requested coverage that does not match the property description. Do not issue coverage based on incomplete property information.  
Coverage must align with the actual risk. Mismatched information may create claim handling concerns.  
Document the requested coverage and property description. Record the resolved coverage treatment.  

**550.CA** Refer risks with prior underwriting correspondence indicating unresolved concerns. Do not bind until the concerns are addressed.  
Prior concerns may remain material to acceptability. The current decision must account for available information.  
Document the unresolved concern and resolution efforts. Record the final disposition.  

**550.CB** Refer risks requiring nonstandard handling not addressed by routine underwriting practice. Do not create an informal exception.  
Nonstandard situations require authority review. Controlled handling supports consistent decisions.  
Document the risk characteristic requiring special handling. Record the authority direction.  

**550.CC** Maintain a clear underwriting record for any North Carolina exception action. Refer uncertainty rather than making unsupported assumptions.  
Clear records support audit, renewal, and claim handling. Referral protects delegated authority standards.  
Document the facts reviewed, decision made, and authority involved. Retain supporting information in the underwriting file.

## Rule 560 — Colorado State Exceptions

**560.A** Confirm that the insured location is eligible for Colorado placement. Decline locations that present an unverified territory condition.

Use verified location data to support territorial underwriting. Avoid assumptions based on mailing information.

Document the verified risk address in the underwriting file. Record the source used for verification.

**560.B** Confirm the occupancy represented at submission. Refer any occupancy that is unclear or inconsistent with the application.

Use occupancy information to identify exposure characteristics. Avoid binding when use of the premises cannot be determined.

Document the stated occupancy and supporting facts. Record any referral disposition.

**560.C** Confirm the named insured has an insurable interest in the dwelling. Refer any ownership arrangement that is not readily supported.

Use ownership review to align the policy with the risk. Avoid issuing coverage for an unsupported interest.

Document the ownership representation in the file. Retain available supporting evidence.

**560.D** Obtain prior loss information before binding. Refer any risk with loss information that suggests unresolved conditions.

Use loss history to identify recurring or uncorrected hazards. Avoid relying solely on applicant explanations.

Document reported losses and underwriting disposition. Record any corrective action represented by the applicant.

**560.E** Require a roof inspection before binding when the reported roof age is at or above 12 years. Refer any risk when the inspection cannot support continued roof eligibility.

Use roof inspection results to evaluate remaining serviceability. Avoid accepting unsupported roof condition statements.

Document the inspection source and findings. Record the roof condition used for the decision.

**560.F** Decline any risk when the reported roof age is at or above 22 years. Do not override this declination point without approved exception handling.

Use the declination point to control deteriorated roof exposure. Avoid binding risks outside stated appetite.

Document the reported roof age and declination action. Record the source of the roof age information.

**560.G** Apply the roof schedule trigger age when reviewing roof condition. Refer any submission that lacks information needed to apply the roof schedule trigger age.

Use the roof schedule trigger age to maintain consistent roof review. Avoid substituting subjective judgment for required review.

Document the roof information considered during underwriting. Record the resulting roof action.

**560.H** Refer any risk with visible roof deterioration, damage, or active leakage. Do not bind until acceptable corrective action is supported.

Use roof condition review to limit water intrusion exposure. Avoid accepting assurances without evidence.

Document the reported condition and corrective action. Retain submitted inspection material when available.

**560.I** Refer any risk with roof material that presents elevated weather susceptibility. Do not bind unless the material and condition are acceptable.

Use roof material review to assess expected performance. Avoid assuming all materials perform alike.

Document the roof material and condition. Record the underwriting decision.

**560.J** Refer any risk with recent roof repair that appears incomplete or temporary. Require support that the repair addresses the reported condition.

Use repair review to distinguish completed work from deferred maintenance. Avoid accepting temporary mitigation as permanent correction.

Document the repair description and available evidence. Record any binding condition imposed.

**560.K** Refer any risk with wildfire exposure concerns. Do not bind until access, vegetation, and defensible condition are acceptable.

Use wildfire review to identify loss severity drivers. Avoid relying on general area descriptions.

Document the wildfire factors reviewed. Record any mitigation represented by the applicant.

**560.L** Refer any risk with unmanaged vegetation near insured structures. Require acceptable clearance before binding.

Use vegetation review to evaluate fire spread potential. Avoid accepting unsupported maintenance statements.

Document the observed or reported vegetation condition. Record required mitigation and follow-up.

**560.M** Refer any risk with steep, unstable, or obstructed access. Do not bind when emergency access cannot be reasonably evaluated.

Use access review to assess emergency response limitations. Avoid assuming routine access is available in adverse conditions.

Document the access characteristics reviewed. Record the source of the information.

**560.N** Refer any risk with private road, shared drive, or access responsibility concerns. Confirm that access arrangements do not impair underwriting acceptability.

Use access responsibility review to identify maintenance uncertainty. Avoid relying on informal representations alone.

Document the reported access arrangement. Record any condition affecting eligibility.

**560.O** Confirm available fire protection information for the risk. Refer any risk when protection information is missing or inconsistent.

Use protection information to assess response capability. Avoid estimating response conditions.

Document the protection information considered. Record the verification source.

**560.P** Refer any risk with hydrant access concerns. Do not bind until the condition is evaluated for acceptability.

Use hydrant access review to identify protection limitations. Avoid accepting generalized statements about nearby service.

Document the reported hydrant condition. Record the underwriting disposition.

**560.Q** Refer any risk with water supply concerns affecting fire protection. Require adequate information before binding.

Use water supply review to assess suppression capability. Avoid assuming supply adequacy from location alone.

Document the water supply information obtained. Record the source and decision.

**560.R** Confirm that the dwelling has an operable heating system appropriate for the occupancy. Refer any risk with heating conditions that create freeze exposure.

Use heating review to identify plumbing damage potential. Avoid accepting vague statements regarding winter use.

Document the heating system description. Record any occupancy or maintenance representation.

**560.S** Refer any risk with prior plumbing leakage, repeated backup, or unresolved moisture condition. Do not bind until corrective action is adequately supported.

Use plumbing review to identify recurring water damage exposure. Avoid accepting repairs that do not address the cause.

Document the reported condition and repair status. Record all underwriting requirements.

**560.T** Refer any vacant or substantially unoccupied dwelling. Do not bind until vacancy controls and property condition are acceptable.

Use occupancy review to identify maintenance and loss detection concerns. Avoid treating an unattended dwelling as routinely occupied.

Document the occupancy status and monitoring arrangements. Record the binding decision.

**560.U** Refer any seasonal dwelling with limited supervision. Confirm that maintenance arrangements support the requested coverage.

Use seasonal review to identify delayed loss discovery exposure. Avoid relying on occasional visits as a control.

Document the supervision and maintenance arrangements. Record any underwriting condition.

**560.V** Refer any risk involving short-term rental activity. Do not bind until the activity and exposure controls are understood.

Use rental activity review to identify occupancy turnover concerns. Avoid accepting a dwelling submission without complete use information.

Document the stated rental activity. Record the underwriting disposition.

**560.W** Refer any risk involving business activity at the insured location. Confirm that the activity does not exceed personal lines appetite.

Use business-use review to identify liability and property exposure. Avoid treating commercial operations as incidental without support.

Document the business activity described. Record the eligibility determination.

**560.X** Refer any risk with animals that present heightened liability exposure. Do not bind until the animal information is complete and acceptable.

Use animal review to identify potential injury exposure. Avoid accepting incomplete animal representations.

Document the animal information reviewed. Record any underwriting restriction or referral action.

**560.Y** Refer any risk with a swimming pool, spa, or similar water feature. Confirm that safety controls are acceptable before binding.

Use water feature review to identify injury exposure. Avoid assuming protections are present without support.

Document the water feature and reported controls. Record the underwriting decision.

**560.Z** Refer any risk with a trampoline, climbing structure, or comparable recreational feature. Confirm that the exposure is acceptable for the requested coverage.

Use recreational feature review to identify injury potential. Avoid accepting unverified safety representations.

Document the feature and reported controls. Record the resulting action.

**560.AA** Refer any risk with solar equipment installed on the dwelling or premises. Confirm that installation, ownership, and maintenance information are acceptable.

Use solar equipment review to identify property and electrical exposure. Avoid assuming equipment is owned by the named insured.

Document the equipment details and ownership representation. Record the underwriting determination.

**560.AB** Refer any risk with energy storage equipment. Do not bind until location, installation, and condition are acceptable.

Use energy storage review to identify fire and electrical concerns. Avoid accepting unsupported installation statements.

Document the equipment information reviewed. Record any referral decision.

**560.AC** Refer any risk with wood-burning, pellet, or similar solid-fuel heating equipment. Confirm that the equipment is maintained and safely installed.

Use solid-fuel review to identify fire and smoke exposure. Avoid accepting an unverified installation.

Document the equipment type and reported maintenance. Record the underwriting action.

**560.AD** Refer any risk with electrical conditions indicating overload, damage, or obsolete components. Do not bind until the condition is resolved or accepted.

Use electrical review to identify fire potential. Avoid accepting repairs without evidence of completion.

Document the reported electrical condition. Record corrective action and disposition.

**560.AE** Refer any risk with foundation movement, structural cracking, or earth movement concerns. Do not bind until the condition is evaluated.

Use structural review to identify progressive damage exposure. Avoid relying on cosmetic descriptions when movement is reported.

Document the reported structural condition. Record the source of the information.

**560.AF** Refer any risk with prior claims that indicate a pattern of preventable loss. Require evidence that the underlying condition has been addressed.

Use claim pattern review to identify recurring exposure. Avoid accepting unsupported assurances of correction.

Document the claim pattern and corrective action. Record the final underwriting decision.

**560.AG** Refer any risk with prior cancellation, nonrenewal, or lapse information that is not fully explained. Do not bind until the reported circumstance is understood.

Use prior coverage review to identify eligibility concerns. Avoid relying on incomplete application responses.

Document the prior coverage information. Record the explanation and disposition.

**560.AH** Refer any submission with material inconsistencies, omissions, or credibility concerns. Do not bind while material facts remain unresolved.

Use submission review to maintain reliable underwriting data. Avoid curing material gaps through assumptions.

Document the inconsistency identified. Record the resolution or declination action.

**560.AI** Do not bind Coverage A above $850,000 without appropriate authority. Refer the risk when the requested limit exceeds available line authority.

Use authority controls to maintain approved underwriting accountability. Avoid splitting or restructuring limits to circumvent authority.

Document the requested Coverage A and referral action. Record the approving authority when approval is granted.

**560.AJ** Apply the wind deductible ceiling when reviewing requested deductible options. Refer any requested wind deductible that exceeds approved handling.

Use deductible review to maintain consistent catastrophe participation. Avoid accepting an unsupported deductible selection.

Document the requested wind deductible and disposition. Record any approved exception.

**560.AK** Refer any risk with prior wind damage that has not been fully repaired. Do not bind until completed repairs are supported.

Use wind damage review to identify residual property exposure. Avoid accepting temporary repairs as final restoration.

Document the reported wind damage and repair status. Record available supporting material.

**560.AL** Refer any risk with hail damage concerns affecting the roof or exterior. Confirm that the condition does not impair eligibility.

Use hail review to identify concealed deterioration potential. Avoid relying solely on a general condition statement.

Document the reported hail condition. Record the underwriting determination.

**560.AM** Refer any risk with siding, exterior finish, or façade damage. Do not bind until the scope and repair status are understood.

Use exterior review to identify weather-related deterioration. Avoid accepting partial repair information as complete.

Document the exterior condition and repair status. Record any binding condition.

**560.AN** Refer any risk with drainage conditions directing water toward the dwelling. Require acceptable correction before binding.

Use drainage review to identify water intrusion potential. Avoid treating visible grading concerns as minor.

Document the drainage condition described. Record the corrective action required.

**560.AO** Refer any risk with a history of sewer backup or drainage overflow. Confirm that the cause and remediation are acceptable.

Use backup review to identify recurring water damage exposure. Avoid accepting cleanup activity as remediation.

Document the prior event and reported corrective action. Record the decision.

**560.AP** Refer any risk with retaining walls, hillside construction, or slope concerns. Do not bind until structural and drainage conditions are acceptable.

Use site review to identify earth movement and water runoff exposure. Avoid relying on broad location descriptions.

Document the site characteristics reviewed. Record the underwriting disposition.

**560.AQ** Refer any risk with construction activity at the insured location. Do not bind until the scope, occupancy, and safeguards are acceptable.

Use construction review to identify changing property exposure. Avoid treating an active project as routine maintenance.

Document the construction activity and occupancy status. Record any referral outcome.

**560.AR** Refer any risk with unfinished areas used for storage, occupancy, or business activity. Confirm that the use is consistent with personal lines appetite.

Use unfinished area review to identify concealed hazards. Avoid accepting broad descriptions without use details.

Document the area use and condition. Record the eligibility decision.

**560.AS** Refer any risk with detached structures used for commercial, agricultural, or public activity. Do not bind until the use is evaluated.

Use detached structure review to identify nonpersonal exposure. Avoid assuming a structure is incidental based on appearance.

Document the structure use and condition. Record the underwriting action.

**560.AT** Refer any risk with substantial personal property kept in detached structures. Confirm that the storage use is acceptable.

Use storage review to identify accumulation and fire exposure. Avoid accepting vague descriptions of contents.

Document the reported storage use. Record the disposition.

**560.AU** Refer any risk with ineligible maintenance conditions, including accumulated debris or unrepaired damage. Require satisfactory correction before binding.

Use maintenance review to identify preventable loss conditions. Avoid accepting promises without support.

Document the maintenance condition and corrective action. Record the follow-up result.

**560.AV** Refer any risk with evidence of water intrusion, mold, or persistent moisture. Do not bind until the source and remediation are understood.

Use moisture review to identify hidden damage potential. Avoid treating surface cleanup as complete remediation.

Document the reported condition and remediation status. Record the underwriting decision.

**560.AW** Refer any risk with burglary, vandalism, or malicious damage history. Confirm that reasonable protective measures are in place.

Use security review to identify recurring property loss exposure. Avoid assuming prior events were isolated without support.

Document the reported loss history and safeguards. Record the final action.

**560.AX** Refer any risk with alarm, monitoring, or protective device representations that affect underwriting. Verify the representation when material to the decision.

Use protective device review to maintain accurate risk classification. Avoid granting underwriting consideration without support.

Document the protective device information. Record the verification source when obtained.

**560.AY** Refer any risk with a dwelling undergoing sale, transfer, or ownership dispute. Do not bind until insurable interest and occupancy are clear.

Use transfer review to identify coverage and maintenance uncertainty. Avoid binding based on anticipated ownership.

Document the ownership status and occupancy representation. Record the underwriting disposition.

**560.AZ** Refer any risk with a trust, estate, entity, or complex ownership arrangement. Confirm that the named insured and insurable interest are acceptable.

Use ownership review to maintain correct policy issuance. Avoid relying on abbreviated ownership descriptions.

Document the ownership arrangement. Record the basis for eligibility.

**560.BA** Refer any risk with a prior inspection identifying unresolved hazards. Do not bind until the hazards are corrected or otherwise accepted.

Use inspection review to identify known conditions requiring action. Avoid disregarding prior adverse findings.

Document the inspection findings and corrective action. Record the final decision.

**560.BB** Refer any risk when photographs are inconsistent with the application. Resolve material differences before binding.

Use image review to validate represented property condition. Avoid treating conflicting information as immaterial.

Document the discrepancy and resolution. Retain relevant submitted images.

**560.BC** Refer any risk with an applicant-requested exception to normal eligibility handling. Do not bind without approved exception authority.

Use exception controls to preserve underwriting discipline. Avoid informal approvals or undocumented deviations.

Document the exception request and decision. Record the approving authority.

**560.BD** Refer any risk with incomplete application responses affecting property condition, occupancy, or loss history. Do not bind while material information is missing.

Use application review to maintain reliable underwriting records. Avoid completing missing facts through inference.

Document the missing information requested. Record the response and disposition.

**560.BE** Refer any risk with a material change reported before policy issuance. Reevaluate eligibility using the changed information.

Use change review to prevent binding on outdated facts. Avoid relying on an earlier representation after conditions change.

Document the reported change. Record the revised underwriting decision.

**560.BF** Refer any risk with a material change reported after binding and before issuance. Apply appropriate underwriting action without delay.

Use post-bind review to maintain accurate risk acceptance. Avoid allowing a material change to remain unresolved.

Document the change and action taken. Record all communications in the file.

**560.BG** Refer any risk requiring special handling due to unusual property characteristics. Do not bind until the exposure is fully evaluated.

Use special handling to address conditions outside routine processing. Avoid applying standard assumptions to unusual risks.

Document the unusual characteristic. Record the underwriting rationale and disposition.

**560.BH** Maintain a complete Colorado underwriting file for every referred or declined risk. Record the facts, sources, conditions, and authority supporting the action.

Use complete file documentation to support consistent underwriting decisions. Avoid undocumented verbal determinations.

Document the final disposition promptly. Retain material underwriting support in the file.

## Rule 570 — Illinois State Exceptions

**570.A** Refer risks with incomplete Illinois location data. Accurate location data supports territory review and hazard evaluation.

Document the verified risk location and the source used to confirm it. Record any unresolved location discrepancy in the file.

**570.B** Refer risks with an ownership interest that cannot be verified. Unclear ownership can impair eligibility and claims handling.

Document the ownership information reviewed and the source of verification. Record the referral outcome.

**570.C** Refer risks with occupancy information that is inconsistent across submitted records. Occupancy affects hazard selection and coverage suitability.

Document the conflicting occupancy information and the clarification obtained. Retain the supporting material in the file.

**570.D** Do not bind Coverage A above $700,000. Limits beyond line authority require underwriting review.

Document the requested Coverage A limit and the referral disposition. Retain approval evidence before binding.

**570.E** Refer any risk requesting a water backup limit above $15,000. Elevated water backup exposure requires review of loss potential.

Document the requested water backup limit and the reason for the requested coverage. Record the underwriter decision.

**570.F** Review the sewer backup limit for consistency with the described premises. The requested coverage must align with the reported exposure.

Document the sewer backup limit considered and the exposure information reviewed. Record any underwriting action taken.

**570.G** Refer risks with prior water damage that remains unrepaired. Unresolved water damage can indicate continuing loss exposure.

Document the reported damage, repair status, and supporting evidence. Record the referral outcome.

**570.H** Refer risks with recurring water intrusion. Recurrent conditions can produce avoidable future loss.

Document the source of intrusion and the corrective action reported. Retain inspection material when available.

**570.I** Refer risks with plumbing conditions that are reported as deteriorated or defective. Deficient plumbing can increase water loss frequency.

Document the condition described and the repair plan provided. Record any binding restriction.

**570.J** Refer risks with roof conditions that are reported as deteriorated, damaged, or unrepaired. Roof condition materially affects weather loss exposure.

Document the roof information reviewed and any repair evidence supplied. Record the underwriting determination.

**570.K** Refer risks with exterior conditions that suggest deferred maintenance. Deferred maintenance can affect property protection and loss severity.

Document the observed condition and the source of the information. Record required corrective action.

**570.L** Refer risks with electrical conditions that are reported as unsafe or deficient. Electrical hazards can create substantial fire exposure.

Document the reported condition and any remediation information. Retain the referral decision.

**570.M** Refer risks with heating equipment that is reported as unsafe, unmaintained, or improperly installed. Heating conditions can contribute to fire and freeze losses.

Document the equipment condition and the corrective action reported. Record any underwriting requirement.

**570.N** Refer risks with solid-fuel heating exposure. This exposure requires review of installation, maintenance, and surrounding conditions.

Document the heating source and the information supporting acceptability. Record the referral outcome.

**570.O** Refer risks with a history of fire loss. Prior fire loss may indicate conditions requiring further underwriting review.

Document the cause of loss, repair status, and mitigation reported. Retain loss information in the file.

**570.P** Refer risks with a history of theft loss involving unresolved security concerns. Unaddressed security conditions can increase repeat-loss potential.
